  `## 🔐 Security & Privacy`
- **01:06**: Modified line 229:
  ``
- **01:06**: Modified line 230:
  `- API keys stored in encrypted DataStore`
- **01:06**: Modified line 231:
  `- Sensitive data not logged`
- **01:06**: Modified line 232:
  `- Optional offline mode (Vosk STT only)`
- **01:06**: Modified line 233:
  `- All network requests over HTTPS`
- **01:06**: Modified line 234:
  `- No data retention on servers`
- **01:06**: Modified line 235:
  ``

- **01:06**: Modified line 237:
  ``
- **01:06**: Modified line 238:
  `- `RECORD_AUDIO` - Capture call audio`
- **01:06**: Modified line 239:
  `- `MODIFY_AUDIO_SETTINGS` - Control audio routing`
- **01:06**: Modified line 240:
  `- `INTERNET` - Cloud API access`
- **01:06**: Modified line 241:
  `- `ANSWER_PHONE_CALLS` - Handle incoming calls`
- **01:06**: Modified line 242:
  `- `READ_CALL_LOG` - Monitor call state`
- **01:06**: Modified line 243:
  `- `PROCESS_OUTGOING_CALLS` - Detect outgoing calls`

  `## 🐛 Troubleshooting`
- **01:06**: Modified line 246:
  ``
- **01:06**: Modified line 247:
  `### Issue: "API Key Invalid"`
- **01:06**: Modified line 248:
  `**Solution**: Verify your API key format. Each provider has specific requirements:`
- **01:06**: Modified line 249:
  `- OpenAI: starts with `sk-``
- **01:06**: Modified line 250:
  `- Grok: starts with `xai-``
- **01:06**: Modified line 251:
  `- Check credential permissions`
- **01:06**: Modified line 252:
  ``
- **01:06**: Modified line 253:
  `### Issue: No Audio Capture`
- **01:06**: Modified line 254:
  `**Solution**: `
- **01:06**: Modified line 255:
  `1. Check microphone permissions`
- **01:06**: Modified line 256:
  `2. Ensure `RECORD_AUDIO` permission granted`
- **01:06**: Modified line 257:
  `3. Verify audio source selection`
- **01:06**: Modified line 258:
  ``
- **01:06**: Modified line 259:
  `### Issue: High Latency`
- **01:06**: Modified line 260:
  `**Solution**:`
- **01:06**: Modified line 261:
  `- Use offline STT (Vosk)`
- **01:06**: Modified line 262:
  `- Switch to Groq for faster LLM`
- **01:06**: Modified line 263:
  `- Use `tts-1` instead of `tts-1-hd``
- **01:06**: Modified line 264:
  ``
- **01:06**: Modified line 265:

- **01:06**: Modified line 266:
  `**Solution**:`
- **01:06**: Modified line 267:
  `- Check Android Studio logcat for errors`
- **01:06**: Modified line 268:
  `- Ensure all providers initialized`
- **01:06**: Modified line 269:
  `- Verify API keys are valid`

  `## 🚀 Что нового`

  `## 🚀 Что нового`
- **01:39**: Modified line 7:
  `- **Модульность**: Проект разделен на `services`, `utils` и `routes` для легкого редактирования.`

  `- **Реальные котировки**: Используется бесплатное CoinGecko API.`
- **01:39**: Modified line 9:
  `- **Авто-наполнение**: При запуске база наполняется популярными валютами (BTC, ETH, USDT, SOL).`

- **01:39**: Modified line 226:
  ``
- **01:39**: Modified line 227:
  `- ✅ JWT токены для аутентификации`

  `- ✅ Проверка прав администратора`
- **01:39**: Modified line 229:

- **01:39**: Modified line 230:

- **01:39**: Modified line 231:

- **01:39**: Modified line 232:
  `  - Используйте HTTPS`
- **01:39**: Modified line 233:
  `  - Настройте CORS правильно`
- **01:39**: Modified line 234:
  `  - Включите валидацию Telegram initData`

  `  - Используйте переменные окружения из безопасного хранилища`
- **01:39**: Modified line 236:
  ``

- **01:39**: Modified line 259:

- **01:39**: Modified line 260:
